ATENÇÃO: material de referência. Revise o ambiente, o escopo e os parâmetros antes de executar comandos destrutivos.

GRANT "ROLE_INFOSAUDE" TO "INFOSAUDE" WITH ADMIN OPTION;
   GRANT "ROLE_INFODEVELOPER" TO "INFOSAUDE" WITH ADMIN OPTION;
   GRANT "ROLE_CRIARUSUARIOS" TO "INFOSAUDE" WITH ADMIN OPTION;
   GRANT "ROLE_SALUXBI" TO "INFOSAUDE" WITH ADMIN OPTION;

   ALTER USER "INFOSAUDE" DEFAULT ROLE ALL;

  GRANT CREATE JOB TO "INFOSAUDE";
  GRANT DEBUG ANY PROCEDURE TO "INFOSAUDE";
  GRANT DEBUG CONNECT SESSION TO "INFOSAUDE";
  GRANT ADMINISTER DATABASE TRIGGER TO "INFOSAUDE";
  GRANT CREATE DATABASE LINK TO "INFOSAUDE";
  GRANT DROP USER TO "INFOSAUDE";
  GRANT ALTER USER TO "INFOSAUDE";
  GRANT CREATE USER TO "INFOSAUDE";
  GRANT CREATE SESSION TO "INFOSAUDE" WITH ADMIN OPTION;
  GRANT ALTER SYSTEM TO "INFOSAUDE";

  GRANT SELECT ON "SYS"."V_$SESSION" TO "INFOSAUDE";
  GRANT SELECT ON "SYS"."V_$LOCK" TO "INFOSAUDE";
  GRANT SELECT ON "SYS"."V_$SQL" TO "INFOSAUDE";
  GRANT SELECT ON "SYS"."DBA_DB_LINKS" TO "INFOSAUDE";
  GRANT SELECT ON "SYS"."DBA_USERS" TO "INFOSAUDE";
  GRANT SELECT ON "SYS"."DBA_DATAPUMP_JOBS" TO "INFOSAUDE";
  GRANT EXECUTE ON "SYS"."DBMS_CRYPTO" TO "INFOSAUDE";

  GRANT SELECT ON "INFOSAUDE"."VW_MAPA_LEITOS" TO "CONSULTA_VW";

  GRANT SELECT ON "INFOSAUDE"."VW_EXAMES" TO "CONSULTA_VW";

  GRANT SELECT ON "INFOSAUDE"."VW_TX_OCUPACAO" TO "CONSULTA_VW";
  GRANT SELECT ON "INFOSAUDE"."VW_ATENDIMENTO" TO "CONSULTA_VW";

   GRANT "EXP_FULL_DATABASE" TO "BACKUP";
   GRANT "IMP_FULL_DATABASE" TO "BACKUP";

   ALTER USER "BACKUP" DEFAULT ROLE ALL;

  GRANT CREATE SESSION TO "BACKUP";

  GRANT SELECT ON "SYS"."DBA_DATAPUMP_JOBS" TO "BACKUP";


   GRANT "EXP_FULL_DATABASE" TO "INFOSAUDE";
   GRANT "IMP_FULL_DATABASE" TO "INFOSAUDE";
   GRANT "ROLE_INFOSAUDE" TO "INFOSAUDE" WITH ADMIN OPTION;
   GRANT "ROLE_INFODEVELOPER" TO "INFOSAUDE" WITH ADMIN OPTION;
   GRANT "ROLE_CRIARUSUARIOS" TO "INFOSAUDE" WITH ADMIN OPTION;

   ALTER USER "INFOSAUDE" DEFAULT ROLE ALL;

  GRANT CREATE SESSION TO "INFOSAUDE" WITH ADMIN OPTION;
  GRANT CREATE USER TO "INFOSAUDE";
  GRANT ALTER USER TO "INFOSAUDE";
  GRANT DROP USER TO "INFOSAUDE";
  GRANT CREATE DATABASE LINK TO "INFOSAUDE";
  GRANT DEBUG CONNECT SESSION TO "INFOSAUDE";
  GRANT DEBUG ANY PROCEDURE TO "INFOSAUDE";

  GRANT SELECT ON "SYS"."V_$SESSION" TO "INFOSAUDE";
  GRANT SELECT ON "SYS"."DBA_DB_LINKS" TO "INFOSAUDE";
  GRANT SELECT ON "SYS"."DBA_USERS" TO "INFOSAUDE";

  DECLARE
  TEMP_COUNT NUMBER;
  SQLSTR VARCHAR2(200);
BEGIN
  SQLSTR := 'ALTER USER "INFOSAUDE" QUOTA UNLIMITED ON "INFOSAUDE_DATA"';
  EXECUTE IMMEDIATE SQLSTR;
EXCEPTION
  WHEN OTHERS THEN
    IF SQLCODE = -30041 THEN
      SQLSTR := 'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM USER_TABLESPACES
              WHERE TABLESPACE_NAME = ''INFOSAUDE_DATA'' AND CONTENTS = ''TEMPORARY''';
      EXECUTE IMMEDIATE SQLSTR INTO TEMP_COUNT;
      IF TEMP_COUNT = 1 THEN RETURN;
      ELSE RAISE;
      END IF;
    ELSE
      RAISE;
    END IF;
END;
/
